It’s not terrible, I don’t think, but it’s enough that I thought maybe I should ask someone about it.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed needing less sleep lately, or feeling rested with just a few hours of sleep?\n\nPatient: Hmm… no, I don’t think so. But I don’t think I’m feeling grand or anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any period recently where you felt a significant increase in your mood, like being unusually happy or irritable, along with a big increase in your energy or activity level, lasting several days or more?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. At least, I don’t think so.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a time when your mood was noticeably different from usual — maybe unusually cheerful or easily annoyed — along with a clear increase in your energy or activity, even if it only lasted a day or two?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so, no. I’m trying to think if there was anything like that, but nothing really stands out as me being different from my usual self. I can get a little annoyed like anyone, or have a good day, but not with some clear jump in energy or activity. I hope that answers what you’re asking.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period, even just a few hours, where you felt unusually excited, unusually irritable, or so energetic that others might have noticed you were acting differently?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I mean, maybe I’ve had times where I’m a little more keyed up or irritated than usual, but not in a way where someone would say I was acting different. At least, nobody’s ever pointed that out to me. I’m not sure if that’s the kind of thing you mean.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"
